var ctx=cv.getContext('2d'),cx=W2,cy=H2,R=Math.min(W,H)2-30dpr,c=S.radar,cs=getComputedStyle(document.body);
var ink=cs.getPropertyValue('--text').trim(),mut=cs.getPropertyValue('--muted').trim(),line=cs.getPropertyValue('--border').trim();
ctx.clearRect(0,0,W,H);var n=SPH.length,rot=c.rotMath.PI180;
SPH.forEach(function(s){var tg=S.sph[s.id].lv;if(radarAnim[s.id]===undefined)radarAnim[s.id]=tg;radarAnim[s.id]+=(tg-radarAnim[s.id]).12});

var prev=prevSnap();
if(c.ghost&&prev){ctx.save();ctx.strokeStyle=rgba(mut,.65);ctx.lineWidth=1.6dpr;ctx.setLineDash([4dpr,4dpr]);ctx.beginPath();
SPH.forEach(function(s,i){var v=clamp((prev[s.id]1)c.max,0,1),a=rot+inMath.PI2;ictx.lineTo(cx+Math.cos(a)Rv,cy+Math.sin(a)Rv)ctx.moveTo(cx+Math.cos(a)Rv,cy+Math.sin(a)Rv)});ctx.closePath();ctx.stroke();ctx.restore()}
var pts=SPH.map(function(s,i){var v=clamp(radarAnim[s.id]c.max,0,1)pulse,a=rot+inMath.PI2;return{xcx+Math.cos(a)Rv,ycy+Math.sin(a)Rv,ss}});

ctx.shadowColor=rgba(cs.getPropertyValue('--accent').trim(),c.glow100);ctx.shadowBlur=18dpr(c.glow100);
var lg=ctx.createLinearGradient(cx-R,cy-R,cx+R,cy+R);lg.addColorStop(0,cs.getPropertyValue('--violet').trim());lg.addColorStop(.5,cs.getPropertyValue('--accent').trim());lg.addColorStop(1,cs.getPropertyValue('--amber').trim());
ctx.strokeStyle=lg;ctx.lineWidth=c.linedpr;ctx.lineJoin='round';ctx.stroke();ctx.restore();
pts.forEach(function(p){if(c.dot0){ctx.save();ctx.fillStyle=p.s.color;ctx.shadowColor=p.s.color;ctx.shadowBlur=8dpr(c.glow100);ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,c.dotdpr.6,0,Math.PI2);ctx.fill();ctx.restore();
ctx.strokeStyle=cs.getPropertyValue('--panel').trim();ctx.lineWidth=1.5dpr;ctx.beginPath();ctx.arc(p.x,p.y,c.dotdpr.6,0,Math.PI2);ctx.stroke()}});
if(c.labelsc.values){ctx.save();ctx.textAlign='center';ctx.textBaseline='middle';
SPH.forEach(function(s,i){var a=rot+inMath.PI2,lx=cx+Math.cos(a)(R+18dpr),ly=cy+Math.sin(a)(R+18dpr);
if(c.labels){ctx.font='700 '+Math.round(10dpr)+'px system-ui,sans-serif';ctx.fillStyle=ink;ctx.fillText(s.icon,lx,ly-(c.values5dpr0))}

function delSkill(id){

var st=(S.skills[id][]).slice();
var wasCustom=!k.base;
var customIdx=-1;S.skillCustom.forEach(function(x,i){if(x.id===id)customIdx=i});

var overrideCopy=S.skillOverrides[id]JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(S.skillOverrides[id]))null;
var dn=st.filter(function(i){return ik.steps.length}).length;
if(!confirm('Удалить «'+k.name+'»'+(dn' Отмечено шагов '+dn+'.''')))return;
if(wasCustom){if(customIdx=0)S.skillCustom.splice(customIdx,1)}
else{S.skillHidden.push(id);delete S.skillOverrides[id]}
delete S.skills[id];
save();renderSkills();renderRadarMeta();sfx('undo');
toast('Навык удалён',function(){
if(wasCustom&&customCopy){S.skillCustom.splice(Math.min(customIdx,S.skillCustom.length),0,customCopy)}
else{S.skillHidden=S.skillHidden.filter(function(x){return x!==id});if(overrideCopy)S.skillOverrides[id]=overrideCopy}
S.skills[id]=st;save();renderSkills();renderRadarMeta();toast('↩️ Навык возвращён')})}

function iconURL(size){var c=document.createElement('canvas');c.width=c.height=size;var x=c.getContext('2d'),r=size.2;
var g=x.createLinearGradient(0,0,size,size);g.addColorStop(0,'#f7d9e6');g.addColorStop(.45,'#d2b4de');g.addColorStop(1,'#d81b60');
x.beginPath();x.moveTo(r,0);x.arcTo(size,0,size,size,r);x.arcTo(size,size,0,size,r);x.arcTo(0,size,0,0,r);x.arcTo(0,0,size,0,r);x.closePath();x.fillStyle=g;x.fill();
x.strokeStyle='#5c3a6b';x.lineWidth=size.035;x.stroke();var cx=size2,cy=size.46,rr=size.21;x.fillStyle='#fdfafd';
x.beginPath();x.moveTo(cx-rr.85,cy-rr.5);x.lineTo(cx-rr1.05,cy-rr1.5);x.lineTo(cx-rr.25,cy-rr.95);x.closePath();x.fill();
x.beginPath();x.moveTo(cx+rr.85,cy-rr.5);x.lineTo(cx+rr1.05,cy-rr1.5);x.lineTo(cx+rr.25,cy-rr.95);x.closePath();x.fill();
x.beginPath();x.ellipse(cx,cy,rr,rr.9,0,0,7);x.fill();x.fillStyle='#3a2542';
x.beginPath();x.ellipse(cx-rr.38,cy-rr.1,rr.11,rr.16,0,0,7);x.fill();x.beginPath();x.ellipse(cx+rr.38,cy-rr.1,rr.11,rr.16,0,0,7);x.fill();
x.fillStyle='#2e9e5b';x.beginPath();x.ellipse(cx+rr.55,size.79,rr.5,rr.24,-.5,0,7);x.fill();return c.toDataURL('imagepng')}
